      = [-2.25, +3.75] s around the change (8 x 750 ms flash cycles)\n13\t  bin              = 250 ms (image duration) -> 24 bins per trial\n14\t  neural           = dF/F (Allen pipeline), averaged per bin (see notes for events comparison)\n15\t  inputs           = none\n16\t  outputs          = image identity (16), image change (2), running quintile (5),\n17\t                     pupil quintile (5), trial outcome (4)\n18\t'''\n19\timport argparse\n20\timport glob\n21\timport os\n22\timport pickle\n23\timport sys\n24\timport time\n25\timport warnings\n26\tfrom multiprocessing import Pool\n27\t\n28\timport numpy as np\n29\timport pandas as pd\n30\t\n31\twarnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n32\t\n33\tfrom allensdk.brain_observatory.behavior.behavior_project_cache import (\n34\t    VisualBehaviorOphysProjectCache)\n35\t\n36\tCACHE_DIR = '/app/data'\n37\tNWB_GLOB = os.path.join(CACHE_DIR, 'visual-behavior-ophys-1.1.0',\n38\t                        'behavior_ophys_experiments', '*.nwb')\n39\t\n40\t# ---------------------------------------------------------------- parameters\n41\tOFF_START = -2.25       # s relative to change time\n42\tOFF_END = 3.75          # s relative to change time\n43\tBIN_SIZE = 0.25         # s\n44\tN_BINS = int(round((OFF_END - OFF_START) / BIN_SIZE))   # 24\n45\tBIN_EDGES = OFF_START + BIN_SIZE * np.arange(N_BINS + 1)\n46\tBIN_CENTERS = BIN_EDGES[:-1] + BIN_SIZE / 2.0\n47\tFLASH_CYCLE = 0.75      # s, image presentation interval (paper)\n48\tPUPIL_MAX_INTERP_GAP = 0.5   # s, blink gaps shorter than this are interpolated\n49\tN_QUANTILES = 5\n50\tOUTCOMES = ['hit', 'miss', 'false_alarm', 'correct_reject']\n51\tNEURAL_SIGNAL_DESCRIPTION = {\n52\t    'events': ('L0-detected calcium events (AllenSDK BehaviorOphysExperiment.events, column '\n53\t               '\"events\") summed within each 250 ms bin; dF/F and event detection were computed '\n54\t               'by the Allen pipeline; only valid ROIs are released'),\n55\t    'filtered_events': ('L0-detected calcium events smoothed with the SDK causal half-gaussian '\n56\t                        '(filtered_events), summed within each 250 ms bin'),\n57\t    'dff': ('dF/F traces computed by the Allen pipeline (BehaviorOphysExperiment.dff_traces), '\n58\t            'averaged within each 250 ms bin; only valid ROIs are released'),\n59\t}\n60\t\n61\t\n62\tdef get_cache():\n63\t    return VisualBehaviorOphysProjectCache.from_local_cache(\n64\t        cache_dir=CACHE_DIR, use_static_cache=False)\n65\t\n66\t\n67\tdef available_experiment_table(cache):\n68\t    '''Experiment table restricted to experiments whose NWB file is present locally.'''\n69\t    et = cache.get_ophys_experiment_table()\n70\t    avail = sorted(int(os.path.basename(f).split('_')[-1].split('.')[0])\n71\t                   for f in glob.glob(NWB_GLOB))\n72\t    sub = et.loc[et.index.isin(avail)].copy()\n73\t    return sub\n74\t\n75\t\n76\t# ------------------------------------------------------------ binning helpers\n77\tdef bin_sum(values, timestamps, edges_abs):\n78\t    '''Sum `values` (n_signals, n_samples) over bins defined by absolute edges.\n79\t\n80\t    edges_abs: (n_trials, N_BINS+1) absolute times.\n81\t    Returns (n_signals, n_trials, N_BINS).\n82\t    '''\n83\t    csum = np.concatenate([np.zeros((values.shape[0], 1), dtype=np.float64),\n84\t                           np.cumsum(values, axis=1)], axis=1)\n85\t    idx = np.searchsorted(timestamps, edges_abs.ravel())          # (n_trials*(N+1),)\n86\t    idx = idx.reshape(edges_abs.shape)\n87\t    take = csum[:, idx]                                           # (n_signals, n_trials, N+1)\n88\t    return (take[:, :, 1:] - take[:, :, :-1]).astype(np.float32)\n89\t\n90\t\n91\tdef bin_mean_nan(values, timestamps, edges_abs):\n92\t    '''Mean of `values` (1d, may contain NaN) within each bin; NaN if no valid sample.'''\n93\t    valid = ~np.isnan(values)\n94\t    filled = np.where(valid, values, 0.0)\n95\t    csum = np.concatenate([[0.0], np.cumsum(filled)])\n96\t    ccnt = np.concatenate([[0], np.cumsum(valid.astype(np.int64))])\n97\t    idx = np.searchsorted(timestamps, edges_abs.ravel()).reshape(edges_abs.shape)\n98\t    s = csum[idx][:, 1:] - csum[idx][:, :-1]\n99\t    n = ccnt[idx][:, 1:] - ccnt[idx][:, :-1]\n100\t    with np.errstate(invalid='ignore', divide='ignore'):\n101\t        out = np.where(n > 0, s / np.maximum(n, 1), np.nan)\n102\t    return out\n103\t\n104\t\n105\tdef interpolate_short_gaps(t, v, max_gap):\n106\t    '''Linearly interpolate NaN runs shorter than max_gap seconds; leave longer runs NaN.'''\n107\t    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).copy()\n108\t    isn = np.isnan(v)\n109\t    if not isn.any() or isn.all():\n110\t        return v\n111\t    idx = np.arange(len(v))\n112\t    filled = np.interp(idx, idx[~isn], v[~isn])\n113\t    d = np.diff(np.concatenate(([0], isn.astype(np.int8), [0])))\n114\t    starts = np.where(d == 1)[0]\n115\t    ends = np.where(d == -1)[0]\n116\t    out = filled\n117\t    for s0, e0 in zip(starts, ends):\n118\t        # gap duration measured between the bracketing valid samples\n119\t        t0 = t[s0 - 1] if s0 > 0 else t[0]\n120\t        t1 = t[e0] if e0 < len(t) else t[-1]\n121\t        if (t1 - t0) > max_gap or s0 == 0 or e0 >= len(t):\n122\t            out[s0:e0] = np.nan\n123\t    return out\n124\t\n125\t\n126\t# ------------------------------------------------------------ session loading\n127\tdef process_session(args):\n128\t    '''Process one ophys session (all of its imaging planes).\n129\t\n130\t    Returns a dict with per-trial neural matrices and *continuous* behaviour\n131\t    (running speed, pupil diameter) which are discretised globally later.\n132\t    '''\n133\t    session_id, eids, region_map, want_debug, signal = args\n134\t    t_start = time.time()\n135\t    cache = get_cache()\n136\t    out = {'session_id': int(session_id), 'error': ''}\n137\t    try:\n138\t        datasets = [cache.get_behavior_ophys_experiment(int(e)) for e in eids]\n139\t        t_load = time.time() - t_start\n140\t        ds0 = datasets[0]\n141\t\n142\t        # ---------------- trials: go + catch, excluding aborted / auto-rewarded\n143\t        trials = ds0.trials\n144\t        sel = ((trials['go'] | trials['catch']) & (~trials['aborted'])\n145\t               & (~trials['auto_rewarded']))\n146\t        trials = trials[sel]\n147\t        trials = trials[~trials['change_time'].isna()]\n148\t        if len(trials) < 2:\n149\t            out['error'] = 'fewer than 2 go/catch trials'\n150\t            return out\n151\t        change_times = trials['change_time'].values.astype(np.float64)\n152\t        edges_abs = change_times[:, None] + BIN_EDGES[None, :]      # (n_trials, N+1)\n153\t        centers_abs = change_times[:, None] + BIN_CENTERS[None, :]  # (n_trials, N)\n154\t        n_trials = len(change_times)\n155\t\n156\t        # ---------------- neural: L0 detected calcium events, summed per bin\n157\t        neural_planes = []\n158\t        region_per_neuron = []\n159\t        cell_ids = []\n160\t        for ds, eid in zip(datasets, eids):\n161\t            ts = np.asarray(ds.ophys_timestamps, dtype=np.float64)\n162\t            if signal == 'dff':\n163\t                ev = np.vstack(ds.dff_traces['dff'].values).astype(np.float64)\n164\t            elif signal == 'filtered_events':\n165\t                ev = np.vstack(ds.events['filtered_events'].values).astype(np.float64)\n166\t            else:\n167\t                ev = np.vstack(ds.events['events'].values).astype(np.float64)\n168\t            assert ev.shape[1] == ts.shape[0], 'trace/timestamps mismatch'\n169\t            n_per_bin = bin_sum(np.ones((1, ts.shape[0])), ts, edges_abs)   # (1, n_trials, N)\n170\t            binned = bin_sum(ev, ts, edges_abs)        # (n_cells, n_trials, N)\n171\t            if signal == 'dff':\n172\t                # average dF/F within the bin (sum would scale with frame rate)\n173\t                binned = binned / np.maximum(n_per_bin, 1)\n174\t            neural_planes.append(binned)\n175\t            region_per_neuron += [region_map[int(eid)]] * ev.shape[0]\n176\t            cell_ids += list(ds.events.index.values)\n177\t        neural = np.concatenate(neural_planes, axis=0)  # (n_neurons, n_trials, N)\n178\t\n179\t        # ---------------- stimulus: image identity and image change\n180\t        sp = ds0.stimulus_presentations\n181\t        sp = sp[sp['stimulus_block_name'].astype(str).str.contains('change_detection')]\n182\t        shown = sp[sp['image_name'] != 'omitted']\n183\t        flash_start = shown['start_time'].values.astype(np.float64)\n184\t        flash_image = shown['image_name'].values.astype(str)\n185\t        flash_ischange = shown['is_change'].values.astype(bool)\n186\t        # index of the most recent shown-image onset at or before each bin centre\n187\t        j = np.searchsorted(flash_start, centers_abs, side='right') - 1\n188\t        if np.any(j < 0):\n189\t            out['error'] = 'bin before first flash'\n190\t            return out\n191\t        image_name = flash_image[j]                                   # (n_trials, N)\n192\t        since = centers_abs - flash_start[j]\n193\t        # image change: bins inside the 750 ms presentation interval of a change flash\n194\t        image_change = (flash_ischange[j] & (since < FLASH_CYCLE)).astype(np.int64)\n195\t\n196\t        # ---------------- running speed (continuous, discretised globally later)\n197\t        run = ds0.running_speed\n198\t        run_t = run['timestamps'].values.astype(np.float64)\n199\t        run_v = run['speed'].values.astype(np.float64)\n200\t        running = bin_mean_nan(run_v, run_t, edges_abs)               # (n_trials, N)\n201\t\n202\t        # ---------------- pupil diameter (continuous, discretised globally later)\n203\t        eye = ds0.eye_tracking\n204\t        if len(eye) == 0:\n205\t            out['error'] = 'no eye tracking data'\n206\t            return out\n207\t        eye_t = eye['timestamps'].values.astype(np.float64)\n208\t        pupil_area = eye['pupil_area'].values.astype(np.float64)\n209\t        pupil_diam = 2.0 * np.sqrt(pupil_area / np.pi)\n210\t        pupil_diam = interpolate_short_gaps(eye_t, pupil_diam, PUPIL_MAX_INTERP_GAP)\n211\t        pupil = bin_mean_nan(pupil_diam, eye_t, edges_abs)            # (n_trials, N)\n212\t\n213\t        # ---------------- trial outcome (static per trial)\n214\t        outcome = np.full(n_trials, -1, dtype=np.int64)\n215\t        for k, name in enumerate(OUTCOMES):\n216\t            outcome[trials[name].values.astype(bool)] = k\n217\t        if np.any(outcome < 0):\n218\t            out['error'] = 'trial without an outcome label'\n219\t            return out\n220\t\n221\t        # ---------------- trial curation: require complete behaviour\n222\t        keep = (~np.isnan(pupil).any(axis=1)) & (~np.isnan(running).any(axis=1))\n223\t        n_dropped = int((~keep).sum())\n224\t        if keep.sum() < 2:\n225\t            out['error'] = 'fewer than 2 trials with complete behaviour'\n226\t            return out\n227\t\n228\t        out.update(dict(\n229\t            neural=neural[:, keep, :].astype(np.float32),\n230\t            image_name=image_name[keep],\n231\t            image_change=image_change[keep],\n232\t            running=running[keep],\n233\t            pupil=pupil[keep],\n234\t            outcome=outcome[keep],\n235\t            change_times=change_times[keep],\n236\t            go=trials['go'].values.astype(bool)[keep],\n237\t            catch=trials['catch'].values.astype(bool)[keep],\n238\t            regions=np.array(region_per_neuron),\n239\t            cell_ids=np.array(cell_ids),\n240\t            mouse_id=str(ds0.metadata['mouse_id']),\n241\t            session_type=str(ds0.metadata['session_type']),\n242\t            cre_line=str(ds0.metadata['cre_line']),\n243\t            experiment_ids=[int(e) for e in eids],\n244\t            n_trials_dropped=n_dropped,\n245\t            n_trials_total=n_trials,\n246\t            t_load=t_load,\n247\t            t_total=time.time() - t_start,\n248\t        ))\n249\t        if want_debug:\n250\t            out['debug'] = dict(\n251\t                ophys_timestamps=np.asarray(datasets[0].ophys_timestamps, dtype=np.float64),\n252\t                events0=np.vstack(datasets[0].events['events'].values).astype(np.float32),\n253\t                run_t=run_t, run_v=run_v, eye_t=eye_t, pupil_diam=pupil_diam,\n254\t                flash_start=flash_start, flash_image=flash_image,\n255\t                flash_ischange=flash_ischange, keep=keep,\n256\t                lick_times=np.asarray(ds0.licks['timestamps'].values, dtype=np.float64),\n257\t                reward_times=np.asarray(ds0.rewards['timestamps'].values, dtype=np.float64),\n258\t            )\n259\t    except Exception as exc:                                   # pragma: no cover\n260\t        import traceback\n261\t        out['error'] = f'{exc!r}\\n{traceback.format_exc()[-800:]}'\n262\t    return out\n263\t\n264\t\n265\t# ------------------------------------------------------------- discretisation\n266\tdef quantile_bins(values, n_bins=N_QUANTILES):\n267\t    '''Global equal-percentile edges (interior) for a 1-d array of values.'''\n268\t    qs = np.linspace(0, 100, n_bins + 1)[1:-1]\n269\t    return np.percentile(values, qs)\n270\t\n271\t\n272\tdef digitize_with(edges, values):\n273\t    return np.digitize(values, edges).astype(np.int64)\n274\t\n275\t\n276\t# ------------------------------------------------------------------ plotting\n277\tdef plot_processing(sess, data, session_index, outfile):\n278\t    '''Plot every processing step for one session to visually verify the conversion.'''\n279\t    import matplotlib\n280\t    matplotlib.use('Agg')\n281\t    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n282\t\n283\t    dbg = sess['debug']\n284\t    neural = data['neural'][session_index]\n285\t    outputs = data['output'][session_index]\n286\t    onames = data['output_names']\n287\t    ct = sess['change_times']\n288\t    tr = 0 if not sess['catch'].any() else int(np.where(sess['catch'])[0][0])\n289\t    trials_to_plot = [0, 1, tr]\n290\t\n291\t    fig, axes = plt.subplots(6, len(trials_to_plot), figsize=(6 * len(trials_to_plot), 18),\n292\t                             sharex='col')\n293\t    for col, trial in enumerate(trials_to_plot):\n294\t        c = ct[trial]\n295\t        t0, t1 = c + OFF_START, c + OFF_END\n296\t        tt = BIN_CENTERS\n297\t\n298\t        # --- 1: raw events of first plane vs binned neural\n299\t        ax = axes[0, col]\n300\t        ots = dbg['ophys_timestamps']\n301\t        m = (ots >= t0) & (ots <= t1)\n302\t        ev = dbg['events0'][:, m]\n303\t        nshow = min(15, ev.shape[0])\n304\t        for i in range(nshow):\n305\t            ax.plot(ots[m] - c, ev[i] + i * 0.5, lw=0.5, color='k', alpha=0.6)\n306\t        ax.set_title(f'trial {trial}: raw events (first plane, {nshow} cells)')\n307\t        ax.axvline(0, color='r')\n308\t\n309\t        ax = axes[1, col]\n310\t        ax.imshow(neural[trial][:min(30, neural[trial].shape[0])], aspect='auto',\n311\t                  extent=[OFF_START, OFF_END, 0, min(30, neural[trial].shape[0])],\n312\t                  interpolation='nearest', cmap='magma')\n313\t        ax.axvline(0, color='c')\n314\t        ax.set_title('binned neural (events summed / 250 ms)')\n315\t\n316\t        # --- 2: stimulus flashes vs image identity output\n317\t        ax = axes[2, col]\n318\t        fs = dbg['flash_start']\n319\t        sel = (fs >= t0 - 1) & (fs <= t1)\n320\t        for st, im, isch in zip(fs[sel], dbg['flash_image'][sel], dbg['flash_ischange'][sel]):\n321\t            ax.axvspan(st - c, st - c + 0.25, color='red' if isch else 'gray', alpha=0.4)\n322\t        img_idx = outputs[trial][onames.index('image_identity')]\n323\t        ax.step(tt, img_idx, where='mid', color='b')\n324\t        ax.axvline(0, color='r')\n325\t        ax.set_title('flashes (gray=repeat, red=change) + image_identity code')\n326\t\n327\t        # --- 3: image change binary\n328\t        ax = axes[3, col]\n329\t        ax.step(tt, outputs[trial][onames.index('image_change')], where='mid', color='m')\n330\t        for st, isch in zip(fs[sel], dbg['flash_ischange'][sel]):\n331\t            if isch:\n332\t                ax.axvspan(st - c, st - c + FLASH_CYCLE, color='red', alpha=0.2)\n333\t        ax.axvline(0, color='r')\n334\t        ax.set_ylim(-0.2, 1.2)\n335\t        ax.set_title('image_change (shaded = 750 ms after a real change)')\n336\t\n337\t        # --- 4: running raw vs discretised\n338\t        ax = axes[4, col]\n339\t        rt, rv = dbg['run_t'], dbg['run_v']\n340\t        m = (rt >= t0) & (rt <= t1)\n341\t        ax.plot(rt[m] - c, rv[m], color='k', lw=0.8, label='running (cm/s)')\n342\t        ax2 = ax.twinx()\n343\t        ax2.step(tt, outputs[trial][onames.index('running_speed_quintile')], where='mid',\n344\t                 color='g', label='quintile')\n345\t        ax2.set_ylim(-0.5, 4.5)\n346\t        ax.axvline(0, color='r')\n347\t        ax.set_title('running speed raw (black) vs quintile (green)')\n348\t\n349\t        # --- 5: pupil raw vs discretised, plus licks/rewards\n350\t        ax = axes[5, col]\n351\t        et_, pd_ = dbg['eye_t'], dbg['pupil_diam']\n352\t        m = (et_ >= t0) & (et_ <= t1)\n353\t        ax.plot(et_[m] - c, pd_[m], color='k', lw=0.8)\n354\t        ax2 = ax.twinx()\n355\t        ax2.step(tt, outputs[trial][onames.index('pupil_diameter_quintile')], where='mid',\n356\t                 color='purple')\n357\t        ax2.set_ylim(-0.5, 4.5)\n358\t        lt = dbg['lick_times']\n359\t        lt = lt[(lt >= t0) & (lt <= t1)]\n360\t        ax.plot(lt - c, np.full(len(lt), np.nanmin(pd_[m]) if m.any() else 0), '|',\n361\t                color='b', ms=10)\n362\t        rw = dbg['reward_times']\n363\t        rw = rw[(rw >= t0) & (rw <= t1)]\n364\t        ax.plot(rw - c, np.full(len(rw), np.nanmin(pd_[m]) if m.any() else 0), 'd',\n365\t                color='c', ms=8)\n366\t        ax.axvline(0, color='r')\n367\t        oc = int(outputs[trial][onames.index('trial_outcome')][0])\n368\t        ax.set_title(f'pupil raw/quintile; licks(blue) rewards(cyan); outcome={OUTCOMES[oc]}')\n369\t        ax.set_xlabel('time from change (s)')\n370\t\n371\t    fig.suptitle(f\"session {sess['session_id']} ({sess['session_type']}, {sess['cre_line']}), \"\n372\t                 f\"{neural[0].shape[0]} neurons, {len(neural)} trials\")\n373\t    fig.tight_layout()\n374\t    fig.savefig(outfile, dpi=110)\n375\t    plt.close(fig)\n376\t    print(f'  wrote {outfile}')\n377\t\n378\t\n379\t# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- main\n380\tdef main():\n381\t    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)\n382\t    ap.add_argument('outfile', type=str, help='output pickle file')\n383\t    ap.add_argument('--full', action='store_true', default=True, help='process all sessions (default)')\n384\t    ap.add_argument('--sample', action='store_true', help='process only 2 sessions')\n385\t    ap.add_argument('--show-processing', action='store_true',\n386\t                    help='plot every processing step for up to 2 sessions')\n387\t    ap.add_argument('--nproc', type=int, default=16)\n388\t    ap.add_argument('--quantile-scope', type=str, default='global',\n389\t                    choices=['global', 'session'],\n390\t                    help='compute running/pupil percentile bins over the whole dataset or per session')\n391\t    ap.add_argument('--limit', type=int, default=0, help='process only the first N sessions (testing)')\n392\t    ap.add_argument('--neural-signal', type=str, default='dff',\n393\t                    choices=['events', 'dff', 'filtered_events'],\n394\t                    help='neural signal to bin (default: L0 detected calcium events)')\n395\t    args = ap.parse_args()\n396\t\n397\t    t_all = time.time()\n398\t    cache = get_cache()\n399\t    et = available_experiment_table(cache)\n400\t    print(f'experiments with local NWB files: {len(et)}')\n401\t    active = et[~et['passive']].copy()\n402\t    print(f'active-behavior experiments: {len(active)} '\n403\t          f'({active[\"ophys_session_id\"].nunique()} ophys sessions, '\n404\t          f'{active[\"mouse_id\"].nunique()} mice)')\n405\t\n406\t    region_map = active['targeted_structure'].to_dict()\n407\t    region_map = {int(k): str(v) for k, v in region_map.items()}\n408\t\n409\t    groups = active.groupby('ophys_session_id')\n410\t    session_ids = sorted(groups.groups.keys())\n411\t    if args.sample:\n412\t        # one single-plane and one multi-plane session, for a representative sample\n413\t        multi = [s for s in session_ids if len(groups.get_group(s)) > 1]\n414\t        single = [s for s in session_ids if len(groups.get_group(s)) == 1]\n415\t        session_ids = [single[0], multi[0]] if multi else single[:2]\n416\t    if args.limit:\n417\t        session_ids = session_ids[:args.limit]\n418\t    n_debug = 2 if args.show_processing else 0\n419\t    jobs = [(int(s), list(groups.get_group(s).index.values), region_map, i < n_debug,\n420\t             args.neural_signal)\n421\t            for i, s in enumerate(session_ids)]\n422\t    print(f'processing {len(jobs)} sessions with {args.nproc} workers ...', flush=True)\n423\t\n424\t    t0 = time.time()\n425\t    if len(jobs) == 1 or args.nproc <= 1:\n426\t        results = [process_session(j) for j in jobs]\n427\t    else:\n428\t        with python3\n'''\nConvert the Allen Brain Observatory Visual Behavior 2P dataset (AllenSDK\nVisualBehaviorOphysProjectCache) into the decoder dictionary format.\n\nUsage:  python -u /app/convert_data.py <outpicklefile> [--full|--sample] [--show-processing]\n\nDesign (see /app/CONVERSION_NOTES.md Step 5 for full justification):\n  session          = one ophys_session_id (all simultaneously recorded imaging planes merged)\n  trials           = (go | catch) & ~aborted & ~auto_rewarded\n  alignment event  = trials.change_time (real change on go trials, sham change on catch trials)\n  window           = [-2.25, +3.75] s around the change (8 x 750 ms flash cycles)\n  bin              = 250 ms (image duration) -> 24 bins per trial\n  neural           = dF/F (Allen pipeline), averaged per bin (see notes for events comparison)\n  inputs           = none\n  outputs          = image identity (16), image change (2), running quintile (5),\n                     pupil quintile (5), trial outcome (4)\n'''\nimport argparse\nimport glob\nimport os\nimport pickle\nimport sys\nimport time\nimport warnings\nfrom multiprocessing import Pool\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport pandas as os.path.join(CACHE_DIR, 'visual-behavior-ophys-1.1.0',\n                        'behavior_ophys_experiments', '*.nwb')\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------- parameters\nOFF_START = -2.25       # s relative to change time\nOFF_END = 3.75          # s relative to change time\nBIN_SIZE = 0.25         # s\nN_BINS = int(round((OFF_END - OFF_START) / BIN_SIZE))   # 24\nBIN_EDGES = OFF_START + BIN_SIZE * np.arange(N_BINS + 1)\nBIN_CENTERS = BIN_EDGES[:-1] + BIN_SIZE / 2.0\nFLASH_CYCLE = 0.75      # s, image presentation interval (paper)\nPUPIL_MAX_INTERP_GAP = 0.5   # s, blink gaps shorter than this are interpolated\nN_QUANTILES = 5\nOUTCOMES = ['hit', 'miss', 'false_alarm', 'correct_reject']\nNEURAL_SIGNAL_DESCRIPTION = {\n    'events': ('L0-detected calcium events (AllenSDK BehaviorOphysExperiment.events, column '\n               '\"events\") summed within each 250 ms bin; dF/F and event detection were computed '\n               'by the Allen pipeline; only valid ROIs are released'),\n    'filtered_events': ('L0-detected calcium events smoothed with the SDK causal half-gaussian '\n                        '(filtered_events), summed within each 250 ms bin'),\n    'dff': ('dF/F traces computed by the Allen pipeline (BehaviorOphysExperiment.dff_traces), '\n            'averaged within each 250 ms bin; only valid ROIs are released'),\n}\n\n\ndef get_cache():\n    return VisualBehaviorOphysProjectCache.from_local_cache(\n        cache_dir=CACHE_DIR, use_static_cache=False)\n\n\ndef available_experiment_table(cache):\n    '''Experiment table restricted to experiments whose NWB file is present locally.'''\n    et = cache.get_ophys_experiment_table()\n    avail = ------------------------------------------------------------ binning helpers\ndef bin_sum(values, timestamps, edges_abs):\n    '''Sum `values` (n_signals, n_samples) over bins defined by absolute edges.\n\n    edges_abs: (n_trials, N_BINS+1) absolute times.\n    Returns (n_signals, n_trials, N_BINS).\n    '''\n    csum = np.concatenate([np.zeros((values.shape[0], 1), dtype=np.float64),\n                           np.cumsum(values, axis=1)], axis=1)\n    idx = np.searchsorted(timestamps, edges_abs.ravel())          # (n_trials*(N+1),)\n    idx = idx.reshape(edges_abs.shape)\n    take = csum[:, idx]                                           # (n_signals, n_trials, N+1)\n    return (take[:, :, 1:] - take[:, :, :-1]).astype(np.float32)\n\n\ndef bin_mean_nan(values, timestamps, edges_abs):\n    '''Mean of `values` (1d, may contain NaN) within each bin; NaN if no valid sample.'''\n    valid = ~np.isnan(values)\n    filled = np.where(valid, values, 0.0)\n    csum = np.concatenate([[0.0], np.cumsum(filled)])\n    ccnt = np.concatenate([[0], np.cumsum(valid.astype(np.int64))])\n    idx = np.searchsorted(timestamps, edges_abs.ravel()).reshape(edges_abs.shape)\n    s = csum[idx][:, 1:] - csum[idx][:, :-1]\n    n = ccnt[idx][:, 1:] - ccnt[idx][:, :-1]\n    with np.errstate(invalid='ignore', divide='ignore'):\n        out = np.where(n > 0, s / np.maximum(n, 1), np.nan)\n    return out\n\n\ndef interpolate_short_gaps(t, v, max_gap):\n    '''Linearly interpolate NaN runs shorter than max_gap seconds; leave longer runs NaN.'''\n    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).copy()\n    isn = np.isnan(v)\n    if not isn.any() or isn.all():\n        return v\n    idx = np.arange(len(v))\n    filled = np.interp(idx, idx[~isn], v[~isn])\n    d = np.diff(np.concatenate(([0], isn.astype(np.int8), [0])))\n    starts = np.where(d == 1)[0]\n    ends = np.where(d == -1)[0]\n    out = filled\n    for s0, e0 in zip(starts, ends):\n        # gap duration measured between the bracketing valid samples\n        t0 = t[s0 - 1] if s0 > 0 else t[0]\n        t1 = t[e0] if e0 < len(t) else t[-1]\n        if (t1 - t0) > max_gap or s0 == 0 or e0 >= len(t):\n            out[s0:e0] = np.nan\n    return out\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------ session loading\ndef process_session(args):\n    '''Process one ophys session (all of its imaging planes).\n\n    Returns a dict with per-trial neural matrices and *continuous* behaviour\n    (running speed, pupil diameter) which are discretised globally later.\n    '''\n    session_id, eids, region_map, want_debug, signal = args\n    t_start = time.time()\n    cache = get_cache()\n    out = {'session_id': int(session_id), 'error': ''}\n    try:\n        datasets = [cache.get_behavior_ophys_experiment(int(e)) for e in eids]\n        t_load = time.time() - t_start\n        ds0 = datasets[0]\n\n        # ---------------- trials: go + catch, excluding aborted / auto-rewarded\n        trials = ds0.trials\n        sel = ((trials['go'] | trials['catch']) & (~trials['aborted'])\n               & (~trials['auto_rewarded']))\n        trials = trials[sel]\n        trials = trials[~trials['change_time'].isna()]\n        if len(trials) < 2:\n            out['error'] = 'fewer than 2 go/catch trials'\n            return out\n        change_times = trials['change_time'].values.astype(np.float64)\n        edges_abs = change_times[:, None] + BIN_EDGES[None, :]      # (n_trials, N+1)\n        centers_abs = change_times[:, None] + BIN_CENTERS[None, :]  # (n_trials, N)\n        n_trials = len(change_times)\n\n        # ---------------- neural: L0 detected calcium events, summed per bin\n        neural_planes = []\n        region_per_neuron = []\n        cell_ids = []\n        for ds, eid in zip(datasets, eids):\n            ts = np.asarray(ds.ophys_timestamps, dtype=np.float64)\n            if signal == 'dff':\n                ev = np.vstack(ds.dff_traces['dff'].values).astype(np.float64)\n            elif signal == 'filtered_events':\n                ev = np.vstack(ds.events['filtered_events'].values).astype(np.float64)\n            else:\n                ev = np.vstack(ds.events['events'].values).astype(np.float64)\n            assert ev.shape[1] == ts.shape[0], 'trace/timestamps mismatch'\n            n_per_bin = bin_sum(np.ones((1, ts.shape[0])), ts, edges_abs)   # (1, n_trials, N)\n            binned = bin_sum(ev, ts, edges_abs)        # (n_cells, n_trials, N)\n            if signal == 'dff':\n                # average dF/F within the bin (sum would scale with frame rate)\n                binned = binned / np.maximum(n_per_bin, 1)\n            neural_planes.append(binned)\n            region_per_neuron += [region_map[int(eid)]] * ev.shape[0]\n            cell_ids += list(ds.events.index.values)\n        neural = np.concatenate(neural_planes, axis=0)  # (n_neurons, n_trials, N)\n\n        # shown = sp[sp['image_name'] != 'omitted']\n        flash_start = shown['start_time'].values.astype(np.float64)\n        flash_image = shown['image_name'].values.astype(str)\n        flash_ischange = shown['is_change'].values.astype(bool)\n        # index of the most recent shown-image onset at or before each bin centre\n        j = np.searchsorted(flash_start, centers_abs, side='right') - 1\n        if np.any(j < 0):\n            out['error'] = 'bin before first flash'\n            return out\n        image_name = flash_image[j]                                   # (n_trials, N)\n        since = centers_abs - flash_start[j]\n        # image change: bins inside the 750 ms presentation interval of a change flash\n        image_change = (flash_ischange[j] & (since < FLASH_CYCLE)).astype(np.int64)\n\n        # ---------------- running speed (continuous, discretised globally later)\n        run = ds0.running_speed\n        run_t = run['timestamps'].values.astype(np.float64)\n        run_v = run['speed'].values.astype(np.float64)\n        running = bin_mean_nan(run_v, run_t, edges_abs)               # (n_trials, N)\n\n        # ---------------- pupil diameter (continuous, discretised globally later)\n        eye = ds0.eye_tracking\n        if len(eye) == 0:\n            out['error'] = 'no eye tracking data'\n            return out\n        eye_t = eye['timestamps'].values.astype(np.float64)\n        pupil_area = eye['pupil_area'].values.astype(np.float64)\n        pupil_diam = 2.0 * np.sqrt(pupil_area / np.pi)\n        pupil_diam = interpolate_short_gaps(eye_t, pupil_diam, PUPIL_MAX_INTERP_GAP)\n        pupil = bin_mean_nan(pupil_diam, eye_t, edges_abs)            # (n_trials, N)\n\n        # ---------------- (~np.isnan(pupil).any(axis=1)) & (~np.isnan(running).any(axis=1))\n        n_dropped = int((~keep).sum())\n        if keep.sum() < 2:\n            out['error'] = 'fewer than 2 trials with complete behaviour'\n            return out\n\n        out.update(dict(\n            neural=neural[:, keep, :].astype(np.float32),\n            image_name=image_name[keep],\n go=trials['go'].values.astype(bool)[keep],\n            catch=trials['catch'].values.astype(bool)[keep],\n            regions=np.array(region_per_neuron),\n            cell_ids=np.array(cell_ids),\n            mouse_id=str(ds0.metadata['mouse_id']),\n            session_type=str(ds0.metadata['session_type']),\n            cre_line=str(ds0.metadata['cre_line']),\n            experiment_ids=[int(e) for e in eids],\n            n_trials_dropped=n_dropped,\n            n_trials_total=n_trials,\n            t_load=t_load,\n            t_total=time.time() - t_start,\n        ))\n        if events0=np.vstack(datasets[0].events['events'].values).astype(np.float32),\n                run_t=run_t, run_v=run_v, eye_t=eye_t, pupil_diam=pupil_diam,\n                flash_start=flash_start, reward_times=np.asarray(ds0.rewards['timestamps'].values, dtype=np.float64),\n            )\n    except Exception as exc:                                   # pragma: no cover\n        import traceback\n        out['error'] = f'{exc!r}\\n{traceback.format_exc()[-800:]}'\n    return out\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------- discretisation\ndef quantile_bins(values, n_bins=N_QUANTILES):\n    '''Global equal-percentile edges (interior) for a 1-d array of values.'''\n    qs = np.linspace(0, 100, n_bins + 1)[1:-1]\n    return np.percentile(values, qs)\n\n\ndef digitize_with(edges, values):\n    return np.digitize(values, edges).astype(np.int64)\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------------ plotting\ndef plot_processing(sess, data, session_index, outfile):\n    '''Plot every processing step for one session to visually verify the conversion.'''\n    import matplotlib\n pipeline-computed `dff_traces` for each valid ROI. The SDK also provides L0 event detection (`events`/`filtered_events`).\n56\t- **ROI curation is automatic**: `exclude_invalid_rois=True` is the default, so only ROIs that passed the Allen segmentation/classification QC (`valid_roi`) are returned. The NWB files released in the cache contain only valid ROIs anyway.\n57\t- **Time bases**: ophys frames (`ophys_timestamps`), stimulus frames (`stimulus_timestamps`, 60 Hz; running speed lives here), eye-tracking frames (~30 Hz), and event times (licks/rewards/trials/stimulus presentations) are all on a common sync clock in seconds, so they can be aligned directly by interpolating/binning onto the ophys timestamps.\n58\t- **Reference trial alignment** in Allen analysis code is to the stimulus **change time** of each trial with a window of a few seconds before/after; this matches the decoder spec (\"Temporally align based on ophys timestamp\", trials = go + catch).\n59\t\n60\t---\n61\t\n62\t## Step 2: Dataset Exploration\n63\t**Status**: COMPLETE\n64\t\n65\t### Data Structure\n66\t`/app/data` is an AllenSDK *local* (non-static) cloud cache for project `visual-behavior-ophys`, manifest v1.1.0:\n67\t- `visual-behavior-ophys_project_manifest_v1.1.0.json` - manifest listing metadata csvs and data files\n68\t- `visual-behavior-ophys-1.1.0/project_metadata/{behavior_session_table, ophys_session_table, ophys_experiment_table, ophys_cells_table}.csv`\n69\t- `visual-behavior-ophys-1.1.0/behavior_ophys_experiments/behavior_ophys_experiment_<ophys_experiment_id>.nwb` - **284 files**\n70\t\n71\tLoading API (works): `VisualBehaviorOphysProjectCache.from_local_cache(cache_dir='/app/data', use_static_cache=False)`.\n72\t(`use_static_cache=True` fails because this cache is laid out as a LocalCache, not a mounted S3 static cache.)\n73\t\n74\tMetadata tables describe the **entire** published VBO dataset (1936 experiments, 703 ophys sessions, 4782 behavior sessions,\n75\t133066 cells), but only **284 experiments** have NWB files present locally -> the usable dataset is this subset.\n76\t\n77\tPer-experiment data objects used (`bc.get_behavior_ophys_experiment(eid)`):\n78\t- `dff_traces` (n_cells x n_frames dF/F), `events` (`events`, `filtered_events`), `ophys_timestamps` (s)\n79\t- `cell_specimen_table` (all `valid_roi == True`, invalid ROIs already excluded)\n80\t- `trials` (21 cols incl. start_time, stop_time, change_time, initial/change_image_name, go, catch, aborted, auto_rewarded, hit, miss, false_alarm, correct_reject)\n81\t- `stimulus_presentations` (19 cols; blocks: `initial_gray_screen_5min`, `change_detection_behavior`, `natural_movie_one`, `post_behavior_gray_screen_5min`); columns `image_name`, `image_index`, `is_change`, `omitted`, `start_time`, `end_time`, `active`, `is_sham_change`, `flashes_since_change`, `trials_id`\n82\t- `running_speed` (timestamps, speed; ~60 Hz), `eye_tracking` (~30 Hz, incl. `pupil_width/height/area`, `likely_blink`; blink frames are NaN)\n83\t- `licks`, `rewards`, `metadata`\n84\t\n85\tExample experiment 792815735 (Vip, OPHYS_1_images_A, VISp): 27 neurons, 140208 ophys frames at 30.94 Hz (4534 s),\n86\t937 trials (164 go, 24 catch, 749 aborted, 0 auto-rewarded), 13808 stimulus presentations (4806 in the change-detection block),\n87\trunning 270257 samples @59.95 Hz, eye tracking 136036 samples @29.99 Hz with 2.26% likely-blink (NaN) frames.\n88\tFor go+catch trials in that session: `change_time - start_time` = 3.02-8.28 s, `stop_time - change_time` = 4.23-4.25 s (fixed).\n89\t\n90\t### Dataset Size (from data files; local cache subset)\n91\t| Statistic | Value |\n92\t|-----------|-------|\n93\t| Ophys experiments (imaging planes) with NWB | 284 (202 active behavior, 82 passive) |\n94\t| Neurons (total, all 284) | 42,147 |\n95\t| Neurons (total, 202 active-behavior experiments) | 29,444 |\n96\t| Neurons / experiment | mean 148.4, median 66, range 4-666 |\n97\t| Subjects (mice) | 38 |\n98\t| Ophys sessions (unique, active) | 174 (168 single-plane + 6 mesoscope sessions with 3-7 planes) |\n99\t| Active ophys sessions / mouse | mean 4.6, range 2-9 |\n100\t| Cre lines (active experiments) | Slc17a7 107, Sst 62, Vip 33 |\n101\t| Targeted structures (active) | VISp 185, VISl 17 |\n102\t| Session types (active) | OPHYS_1_images_A 55, OPHYS_3_images_A 55, OPHYS_4_images_B 46, OPHYS_6_images_B 46 |\n103\t| Experience levels (active) | Familiar 110, Novel >1 54, Novel 1 38 |\n104\t| Trials / session | ~900 total, of which ~190 go+catch (session-dependent) |\n105\t| Ophys frame rate | ~31 Hz (single plane), ~11 Hz (mesoscope) |\n106\t\n107\t---\n108\t\n109\t## Step 3: Reference Text Reading\n110\t**Status**: COMPLETE\n111\t\n112\t### Expected Statistics (from papers/methods)\n113\t| Statistic | Value | Source Quote |\n114\t|-----------|-------|--------------|\n115\t| Task | go/no-go change detection | \"mice are presented with a continuous series of briefly presented stimuli and they earn water rewards by correctly reporting when the identity of the image changes\" (whitepaper) |\n116\t| Images per session | 8 (64 possible transitions) | \"Each session included 8 images, for a total of 64 possible transitions\" |\n117\t| Stimulus timing | 250 ms image, 500 ms gray (750 ms cycle) | \"natural images (250 ms stimulus duration) interspersed with periods of a gray screen (500 ms inter-stimulus duration)\" (paper) |\n118\t| Omission probability | 5% of flashes (never at a change or the flash before) | \"5% of image repeats were omitted... Image changes as well as the image immediately before the change were not omitted\" |\n119\t| Catch probability | ~12.5% of go/catch trials | \"a matrix sampling algorithm... pushing the actual catch probability to ~12.5%\" |\n120\t| Change time distribution | truncated exponential 2.25-8.25 s after trial start, mean ~4.2 s | \"Change-times were selected from a truncated exponential distribution ranging from 2.25 to 8.25 seconds... resulting in a mean change time of 4.2 seconds\" |\n121\t| Response window | 150-750 ms after the change | \"the fraction of go-trials in which the mouse licked in a 0.150 to 0.750 second window\" |\n122\t| Auto-rewarded trials | 5 at session start + after 10 consecutive misses | \"Behavior sessions across all phases began with 5 free-reward trials... after 10 consecutive MISS trials\" |\n123\t| Ophys frame rate | 31 Hz single plane, 11 Hz per plane multi-plane | \"Two-photon movies (512x512 pixels, 31 Hz for single plane and 512x512 pixels, 11 Hz for each plane in multi-plane experiments)\" |\n124\t| Eye-tracking rate | 30 Hz | \"eye tracking (30 Hz), and behavior (30 Hz)\" |\n125\t| Running-speed processing | zscore>10 transient removal + 10 Hz lowpass Butterworth | \"Any additional transients with z-score of 10 or greater were removed... smoothing the raw running speed with a 10 Hz lowpass Butterworth filter\" |\n126\t| Running speed units | cm/s (wheel radius 16.5 cm/2, 2/3 radius point) | \"running_speed_cm_per_sec = angular_speed * (2/3 * wheel_radius)\" |\n127\t| dF/F | computed by the pipeline (600 s median-filter baseline) | \"DF/F CALCULATION ... median filter with kernel size 600s ... subtract this baseline ... normalize by the baseline\" |\n128\t| ROI curation | invalid ROIs (unions, duplicates, edge, dendrites, too small/dim) excluded by classifier | \"ROI FILTERING ... any ROIs that end up labeled are not considered cell bodies\" |\n129\t| Paper neural signal | detected calcium events | \"For all analysis of neural data we used the detected calcium events\" |\n130\t| Paper session/mouse counts (behavior) | 376 imaging sessions, 82 mice (full public dataset) | \"contains behavior from 376 imaging sessions from 82 mice\" |\n131\t| Paper neural counts (their subset) | 8,619 excitatory (21 sessions, 9 mice), 470 Sst (15 sessions, 6 mice), 1,239 Vip (21 sessions, 9 mice) | \"Our dataset contains 8,619 excitatory cells...\" |\n132\t| Engagement threshold | 2 rewards/minute | \"corresponds to reward rates above and below 2 rewards per minute\" |\n133\t\n134\tNOTE: the paper analysed the *full* public VBO release; the local cache holds only a 284-experiment subset, so absolute\n135\tcounts (mice/sessions/cells) cannot match the paper. Rates/fractions (catch fraction, omission fraction, hit/FA rates,\n136\tchange-time distribution, frame rates) are the statistics that *must* match, and they do (see Step 4/9).\n137\t\n138\t### Processing Details\n139\t- **Temporal alignment**: all streams are on one sync clock (s). Neural data define the time base (`ophys_timestamps`);\n140\t  behavior (running 60 Hz, eye 30 Hz) and stimulus/trial event times are resampled onto the neural time bins.\n141\t- **Trial-aligned analysis in Allen reference code** (swdb/save_trial_response_df.py) aligns to `trials.change_time`\n142\t  with a window of [-4, +8] s at the ophys frame rate.\n143\t- **Paper decoding analysis**: random forest, per *image presentation*, using neural activity in the **first 400 ms after\n144\t  image presentation**; decoded (a) image change vs repeat, (b) hit vs miss; 5-fold CV; accuracy reported as % correct.\n145\t  Reported qualitatively: change decoding is well above chance for all cell classes (Figure 6A, ~60-80% correct), hit/miss\n146\t  decoding modestly above chance (Figure 6C), and false-alarm decoding \"very low\" (Figure S22).\n147\t- **Behavioral data processing (paper)**: behavioral events assigned to the 750 ms *image presentation interval* beginning\n148\t  at each flash (also used for omissions). This motivates using the 750 ms flash cycle as the natural quantum of time.\n149\t\n150\t### Curation Steps\n151\t\n152\t**Neuron curation rules**:\n153\t- ROI filtering already applied by the Allen pipeline (multi-label classifier; unions/duplicates/edge/dendrite/small/dim\n154\t  ROIs marked `valid_roi=False`); the SDK returns only valid ROIs (`exclude_invalid_rois=True` default). No further\n155\t  neuron filtering is described in the paper.\n156\t- Session/container QC (z-drift < 10 um, <20% photobleaching, d-prime >= 1, sync verified, no interictal events) was applied\n157\t  before release, so all released experiments pass QC.\n158\t\n159\t**Trial curation rules**:\n160\t- Trials are go / catch / aborted / auto_rewarded. This conversion keeps **go + catch** and drops **aborted**\n161\t  (mouse licked before the change) and **auto_rewarded** (free reward) trials, per the task specification. This matches\n162\t  the SDK convention that hit/miss/false_alarm/correct_reject are only defined on go/catch trials.\n163\t- Passive sessions (OPHYS_2, OPHYS_5) have no behavioral responses/trial outcomes -> excluded (the paper also excluded\n164\t  passive sessions: \"Imaging was also performed during passive viewing of the same stimulus, which was not analyzed here\").\n165\t\n166\t### Decoders Trained (paper)\n167\t| Decoded variable | Accuracy |\n168\t| image change vs repeat (random forest, 400 ms window) | well above chance, ~0.6-0.85 % correct depending on n cells/cell class (Fig 6A) |\n169\t| hit vs miss (random forest) | modestly above chance (Fig 6C) |\n170\t| false alarm | \"very low\" (Fig S22) |\n171\t\n172\t---\n173\t\n174\t## Step 4: Check for Consistency\n175\t**Status**: COMPLETE\n176\t\n177\tI cross-checked every quantity that the whitepaper/paper state against what the SDK code computes and what the data contain\n178\t(scripts in `/app/cache/`: `scan_all.py`, `check_images.py`, `check_windows.py`, `check_eye.py`, `check_pupil_trials.py`;\n179\toutputs `scan_all.csv`, `check_*.csv`).\n180\t\n181\t### Discrepancies Found\n182\t| Topic | Code says | Data shows | Papers say | Resolution |\n183\t|-------|-----------|------------|------------|------------|\n184\t| Cache loading | `from_local_cache(use_static_cache=True)` needs `<dir>/visual-behavior-ophys/manifests` | cache has manifest json at top level + `visual-behavior-ophys-1.1.0/` data dir | tutorials use `from_s3_cache` (no network here) | Use `from_local_cache(cache_dir='/app/data', use_static_cache=False)`; verified all 284 NWBs are reachable through the SDK |\n185\t| Dataset size | metadata tables cover 1936 experiments / 703 sessions / 133k cells | only 284 NWB files present (202 active) | paper analysed 376 imaging sessions / 82 mice | Local cache is a subset; absolute counts cannot match, **rates** must (and do). Restrict to experiments whose NWB exists |\n186\t| Catch probability | trial types from `trial.py` | catch/(go+catch) = **0.1253** over 202 experiments | \"~12.5%\" (whitepaper) | Consistent |\n187\t| Omission probability | `presentations.py` marks `omitted` | omitted fraction of change-detection flashes = **0.0352** (mean) | \"5% of image repeats were omitted\" | Consistent: 5% applies to *eligible* repeats only - changes, the flash before a change, and the first flashes after a change are never omitted, which lowers the overall rate to ~3.5% |\n188\t| Change times | SWDB asserts change_times are a subset of flash start times | true in **202/202** experiments | change is a flash | Consistent |\n189\t| Change time distribution | - | change-start: min 2.79 s, mean ~4.2 s | \"truncated exponential 2.25-8.25 s, mean 4.2 s\" | Consistent |\n190\t| Images per session | - | exactly 8 non-omitted images in every experiment; 16 unique across the dataset (2 disjoint sets A/B) | \"Each session included 8 images\" | Consistent |\n191\t| Ophys frame rate | - | 30.93-30.95 Hz (168 single-plane experiments), 10.73 Hz (34 mesoscope experiments) | \"31 Hz single plane, 11 Hz per plane multi-plane\" | Consistent |\n192\t| Running speed | `running_processing.get_running_df` (zscore 10 outlier removal + Butterworth lowpass) | `running_speed` @59.95 Hz, no NaNs in any experiment | \"10 Hz lowpass Butterworth\" (code uses order 3, Wn=4 Hz, fs=60) | Use SDK `running_speed` as-is (already the processed, filtered stream) |\n193\t| Eye tracking | `EyeTrackingTable.from_nwb` recomputes blinks and NaNs them | ~3.5% NaN frames/experiment; **3 sessions have no eye-tracking table at all** | \"eye tracking (30 Hz)\" | Blinks -> NaN by design. Handle explicitly (see Step 5 decisions) |\n194\t| Neural signal | SDK provides `dff_traces`, `events`, `filtered_events` | both present for all experiments | \"For all analysis of neural data we used the detected calcium events\" (paper) | Use **`events`** (L0-detected calcium event magnitudes), matching the paper |\n195\t| ROI curation | `exclude_invalid_rois=True` default | `cell_specimen_table.valid_roi` is True for 100% of returned ROIs | ROI FILTERING section | Nothing further to do - released NWBs contain only valid ROIs |\n196\t| Multi-plane sessions | experiments = imaging planes | 6 sessions have 3-7 planes; planes in a session share *identical* trial tables (verified: 0/174 sessions disagree) | \"up to 8 imaging planes (8 experiments) per session\" | Merge planes of the same `ophys_session_id` into ONE decoder session (simultaneously recorded neurons) |\n197\t| Trial outcome labels | `trial.py`: hit/miss/false_alarm/correct_reject defined only for go/catch | every go+catch trial has exactly one of the four | go/no-go task | Use the 4 outcomes as the static per-trial output |\n198\t| Behaviour rates | - | hit rate 0.350, false-alarm rate 0.143 over all included trials | d-prime >= 1 QC criterion | Consistent with well-trained mice (d' ~ 0.5-1.5 range) |\n199\t\n200\tNo unresolved discrepancies.\n201\t\n202\t---\n203\t\n204\t## Step 5: Mapping Planning\n205\t**Status**: COMPLETE\n206\t\n207\t### Session / trial definition\n208\t- **Session** = one `ophys_session_id` (a single continuous recording). For Multiscope sessions the 3-7 simultaneously\n209\t  recorded imaging planes (`ophys_experiment_id`s) are **concatenated along the neuron axis** into one session, because\n210\t  they are simultaneously recorded neurons sharing one behavior session (verified: all planes of a session have identical\n211\t  trial tables). Only **active behavior** sessions are used (passive OPHYS_2/OPHYS_5 have no choices/outcomes).\n212\t- **Trial** = a `trials` row with `(go | catch) & ~aborted & ~auto_rewarded`, per the Decoder Task spec.\n213\t- **Alignment event** = `trials.change_time` (the stimulus change on go trials, the sham change on catch trials).\n214\t  This is the reference event used by the Allen SWDB analysis code (`save_trial_response_df.py`).\n215\t- **Trial window** = **[-2.25 s, +3.75 s]** around the change time = 8 flash cycles (3 before, 5 after).\n216\t  Data-driven justification: over all 202 active experiments min(change_time - start_time) = 2.79 s and\n217\t  min(stop_time - change_time) = 4.20 s, so this window is **always inside the trial** (no bleed into neighbouring trials,\n218\t  no truncation, identical length for every trial) and always inside `ophys_timestamps`.\n219\t- **Time bin** = **250 ms** -> **24 bins/trial**. Chosen because (a) it equals the image presentation duration and divides\n220\t  the 750 ms flash cycle exactly into 3, so image/change labels never straddle bins; (b) it is larger than the slowest\n221\t  ophys frame interval (93 ms on the Mesoscope), so every bin of every session contains >= 2 imaging frames, allowing one\n222\t  common bin size for all sessions as the format requires.\n223\t\n224\t### Variable Mapping\n225\t| Source Variable | Target Field | Transform | Reference Code Function(s) | Notes |\n226\t|-----------------|--------------|-----------|----------------------------|-------|\n227\t| `experiment.events['events']` (L0 detected calcium events) + `ophys_timestamps` | `neural` | sum of event magnitudes of the ophys frames whose timestamp falls in each 250 ms bin -> (n_neurons, 24) float32 | `Events.from_nwb`; paper \"we used the detected calcium events\" | Neurons of all planes of a session stacked |\n228\t| (none) | `input` | empty array (0, 24) float32, `input_names=[]` | - | Decoder Task specifies \"No inputs for this task\" |\n229\t| `stimulus_presentations.image_name` (change_detection block, non-omitted) | `output[0]` image_identity | per bin: image of the most recent non-omitted flash onset <= bin centre; 16 global classes (im000...im106) | tutorials use `stimulus_block_name.str.contains('change_detection')` | Holding the identity across the 500 ms gray ISI implements the paper's \"image presentation interval\" (750 ms starting at each flash); during a 5% omission the identity of the ongoing (repeating) image is unchanged, so it is held |\n230\t| `stimulus_presentations.is_change` | `output[1]` image_change | 1 in the bins of the presentation interval of a change flash (3 bins = 750 ms starting at the change), else 0 | `presentations.py` `is_change` | Catch trials have a *sham* change (`is_change` False) so they stay 0 - exactly the change-vs-repeat discrimination of the paper's change decoder |\n231\t| `running_speed` (`speed`, 60 Hz) | `output[2]` running_speed_quintile | mean speed per 250 ms bin -> discretised into 5 **global** equal-percentile bins (20/40/60/80th pct of all bins in the dataset) | `running_processing.get_running_df` (already filtered by SDK) | Decoder Task: \"discretized into five equal percentile bins\" |\n232\t| `eye_tracking.pupil_area` (30 Hz) | `output[3]` pupil_diameter_quintile | diameter = 2*sqrt(area/pi); blink NaN gaps <= 0.5 s linearly interpolated; mean per bin; 5 global equal-percentile bins | `EyeTrackingTable.from_nwb` (blinks already NaN, z=3, dilation 2) | Diameter (not area) as required |\n233\t| `trials.hit/miss/false_alarm/correct_reject` | `output[4]` trial_outcome | static per trial, broadcast to all 24 bins; 4 classes | `trial.py::_get_trial_data` | Static per-trial output as specified |\n234\t| `ophys_experiment_table.mouse_id` | `subjects`, `subject_idx` | str ids | - | |\n235\t| `ophys_experiment_table.targeted_structure` (per plane) | `brain_regions`, `brain_region_idx` | VISp / VISl per neuron | - | |\n236\t\n237\t### Key Decisions\n238\t1. **Neural signal = detected calcium events (`events`), summed per bin.** The paper states \"For all analysis of neural data\n239\t   we used the detected calcium events\"; events remove the slow GCaMP6f decay, which otherwise smears stimulus information\n240\t   across many 250 ms bins. Raw (unsmoothed) `events` is used rather than `filtered_events`, whose causal half-gaussian is\n241\t   documented in the SDK as a *visualisation* smoother; binning already integrates over 250 ms.\n242\t2. **dF/F is not recomputed** - the Allen pipeline already provides `dff_traces`, and the event detection was run on those\n243\t   traces. ROI curation is likewise already applied (`valid_roi`, `exclude_invalid_rois=True` default): 100% of returned ROIs\n244\t   are valid, so no further neuron filtering is warranted (and none is described in the papers).\n245\t3. **Mesoscope planes merged per session** (simultaneously recorded populations) - gives larger neuron counts and matches the\n246\t   whitepaper definition of a *session* vs an *experiment*.\n247\t4. **Passive sessions excluded** (no behavioral report -> no trial outcome), matching the paper (\"passive viewing ... was not\n248\t   analyzed here\").\n249\t5. **Aborted and auto-rewarded trials excluded** per the Decoder Task spec; this also matches the SDK's own convention that\n250\t   hit/miss/false-alarm/correct-reject are only defined for go/catch trials.\n251\t6. **Global (dataset-wide) quintile edges** for running speed and pupil diameter, so the 5 classes are equally populated over\n252\t   the whole dataset as \"five equal percentile bins\" requires, and so that one shared decoder head sees a consistent labelling.\n253\t7. **Missing pupil data**: 3 of 174 active sessions have *no* eye-tracking table at all -> those sessions are dropped (a\n254\t   required output cannot be fabricated). Within the remaining sessions, blink gaps <= 0.5 s are linearly interpolated (blinks\n255\t   are short); trials that still contain a bin with no valid pupil sample (~6% of trials) are dropped. Fabricating pupil\n256\t   values for a *decoded* variable would corrupt the evaluation.\n257\t8. **All active sessions of all cre lines, both image sets, both areas and all experience levels are kept.** The paper's\n258\t   restriction to familiar/Mesoscope sessions served its scientific question (strategy comparison); for building a decoding\n259\t   dataset, more sessions is strictly better and the task says to convert data collected under the Visual Behavior task.\n260\t9. **Image identity uses the 16 global image names**, not the per-session 0-7 `image_index`, because index i means different\n261\t   images in image sets A and B.\n262\t\n263\t### Planned Sanity Checks\n264\t- [ ] catch/(go+catch) ~ 0.125 (whitepaper ~12.5%)\n265\t- [ ] 8 distinct images present per session; 16 across the dataset\n266\t- [ ] change_time always coincides with a flash start time\n267\t- [ ] image_change == 1 in exactly 3 bins (bins 9-11) of every go trial and 0 for every catch trial\n268\t- [ ] image identity at bin 8 (just before change) != identity at bin 9 for go trials, and == for catch trials\n269\t- [ ] running/pupil quintiles each ~20% of bins\n270\t- [ ] trial-outcome distribution reproduces hit rate ~0.35, FA rate ~0.14\n271\t- [ ] per-neuron binned event sums match an independent re-computation directly from the SDK (spot check)\n272\t- [ ] no NaN/Inf anywhere; all trials have identical shape (n_neurons, 24)\n273\t- [ ] number of trials per session equals the number of go+catch trials in the SDK trials table (minus pupil-dropped trials)\n274\t\n275\t---\n276\t\n277\t## Step 6: Script Development\n278\t**Status**: COMPLETE\n279\t\n280\t`/app/convert_data.py` (runs as `python -u /app/convert_data.py <outfile> [--full|--sample] [--show-processing]`).\n281\t\n282\tStructure:\n283\t- `get_cache()` / `available_experiment_table()` - open the AllenSDK `VisualBehaviorOphysProjectCache`\n284\t  (`from_local_cache(cache_dir='/app/data', use_static_cache=False)`) and restrict the experiment table to experiments\n285\t  whose NWB file is present locally. **All data access goes through the SDK**; no NWB file is opened directly.\n286\t- `process_session((session_id, experiment_ids, region_map, want_debug, signal))` - worker that loads every imaging plane of\n287\t  one ophys session, selects go+catch trials, and bins neural + behaviour into the 24 x 250 ms trial windows.\n288\t- `bin_sum` / `bin_mean_nan` - fully vectorised binning via cumulative sums + `np.searchsorted` (no python loop over trials);\n289\t  `bin_mean_nan` propagates NaN only when a bin contains *no* valid sample.\n290\t- `interpolate_short_gaps` - linear interpolation of pupil blink gaps <= 0.5 s.\n291\t- `quantile_bins` / `digitize_with` - global equal-percentile discretisation of running speed and pupil diameter.\n292\t- `plot_processing` - `--show-processing` figure with 6 rows x 3 trials: raw events/dF/F traces, binned neural matrix,\n293\t  stimulus flashes vs decoded image identity, image-change binary vs the true change flash, raw running speed vs its\n294\t  quintile, raw pupil diameter vs its quintile with licks/rewards and the trial outcome.\n295\t- `main()` - grouping by `ophys_session_id`, multiprocessing pool, global quantile edges, assembly of the output dict,\n296\t  statistics printout, pickling.\n297\t\n298\tCode inefficiencies identified:\n299\t- A naive implementation would slice traces per trial in a Python loop (n_trials x n_cells slices).\n300\t- Re-opening the cache per experiment is expensive.\n301\t\n302\tCode speedups added:\n303\t- Cumulative-sum + `searchsorted` binning: all trials of a session are binned in one vectorised call.\n304\t- One cache handle per worker process, sessions processed in parallel (`--nproc`, default 16).\n305\t- `float32` storage for neural data.\n306\t- Timing is printed per run (`s/session`).\n307\t\n308\tExtra option `--neural-signal {dff,events,filtered_events}` was added so the choice of neural signal could be tested\n309\tempirically (see Step 7/8).\n310\t\n311\t---\n312\t\n313\t## Step 7: Sample Conversion and Validation\n314\t**Status**: COMPLETE\n315\t\n316\t`python -u /app/convert_data.py /app/sample_data.pkl --sample --show-processing` Image changes as well as the image immediately before the change were not omitted\" |\n| Catch probability | ~12.5% of go/catch trials | \"a matrix sampling algorithm... pushing the actual catch probability to ~12.5%\" |\n| Change time distribution | truncated exponential 2.25-8.25 s after trial start, mean ~4.2 s | \"Change-times were selected from a truncated exponential distribution ranging from 2.25 to 8.25 seconds... resulting in a mean change time of 4.2 seconds\" |\n| Response window | 150-750 ms after the change | \"the fraction of go-trials in which the mouse licked in a 0.150 to 0.750 second window\" |\n| Auto-rewarded trials | 5 at session start + after 10 consecutive misses | \"Behavior sessions across all phases began with 5 free-reward trials... after 10 consecutive MISS trials\" |\n| Ophys frame rate | 31 Hz single plane, 11 Hz per plane multi-plane | \"Two-photon movies (512x512 pixels, 31 Hz for single plane and 512x512 pixels, 11 Hz for each plane in multi-plane experiments)\" |\n| Eye-tracking rate | 30 Hz | \"eye tracking (30 Hz), and behavior (30 Hz)\" |\n| Running-speed processing | zscore>10 transient removal + 10 Hz lowpass Butterworth | \"Any additional transients with z-score of 10 or greater were removed... smoothing the raw running speed with a 10 Hz lowpass Butterworth filter\" |\n| Running speed units | cm/s (wheel radius 16.5 cm/2, 2/3 radius point) | \"running_speed_cm_per_sec = angular_speed * (2/3 * wheel_radius)\" |\n| dF/F | computed by the pipeline (600 s median-filter baseline) | \"DF/F CALCULATION ... median filter rewards/minute | \"corresponds to reward rates above and below 2 rewards per minute\" |\n\nNOTE: the paper analysed the *full* public VBO release; the local cache holds only a 284-experiment subset, so absolute\ncounts (mice/sessions/cells) cannot match the paper. Rates/fractions (catch fraction, omission fraction, hit/FA rates,\nchange-time distribution, frame rates) are the statistics that *must* match, and they do (see Step 4/9).\n\n### Processing Details\n- **Temporal alignment**: all streams are on one sync clock (s). Neural data define the time base (`ophys_timestamps`);\n  behavior (running 60 Hz, eye 30 Hz) and stimulus/trial event times are resampled onto the neural time bins.\n- **Trial-aligned analysis in Allen reference code** (swdb/save_trial_response_df.py) aligns to `trials.change_time`\n  with a window of [-4, +8] s at the ophys frame rate.\n- **Paper decoding analysis**: random forest, per *image presentation*, using neural activity in the **first 400 ms after\n  image presentation**; decoded (a) image change vs repeat, (b) hit vs miss; 5-fold CV; accuracy reported as % correct.\n  Reported qualitatively: change decoding is well above chance for all cell classes (Figure 6A, ~60-80% correct), hit/miss\n  decoding modestly above chance (Figure 6C), and false-alarm decoding \"very low\" (Figure S22).\n- **Behavioral data processing (paper)**: behavioral events assigned to the 750 ms *image presentation interval* beginning\n  at each flash (also used for omissions). This motivates using the 750 ms flash cycle as the natural quantum of time.\n\n### Curation Steps\n\n**Neuron curation rules**:\n- ROI filtering already applied by the Allen pipeline (multi-label classifier; unions/duplicates/edge/dendrite/small/dim\n  ROIs marked `valid_roi=False`); the SDK returns only valid ROIs (`exclude_invalid_rois=True` default). No further\n  neuron filtering is described in the paper.\n- Session/container QC (z-drift < 10 um, <20% photobleaching, d-prime >= 1, sync verified, no interictal events) was applied\n  before release, so all released experiments pass QC.\n\n**Trial curation rules**:\n- Trials are go / catch / aborted / auto_rewarded. This conversion keeps **go + catch** and drops **aborted**\n  (mouse licked before the change) and **auto_rewarded** (free reward) trials, per the task specification. This matches\n  the SDK convention that hit/miss/false_alarm/correct_reject are only defined on go/catch trials.\n- Passive sessions (OPHYS_2, OPHYS_5) have no behavioral responses/trial outcomes -> excluded (the paper also excluded\n  passive sessions: \"Imaging was also performed during passive viewing of the same stimulus, which was not analyzed here\").\n\n### Decoders Trained (paper)\n| Decoded variable | Accuracy |\n| image change vs repeat (random forest, 400 ms window) | well above chance, ~0.6-0.85 % correct depending on n cells/cell class (Fig 6A) |\n| hit vs miss (random forest) | modestly above chance (Fig 6C) |\n| false alarm | \"very low\" (Fig S22) |\n\n---\n\n## Step 4: Check for Consistency\n**Status**: COMPLETE\n\nI cross-checked every quantity that the whitepaper/paper state against what the SDK code computes and what the data contain\n(scripts in `/app/cache/`: `scan_all.py`, `check_images.py`, `check_windows.py`, `check_eye.py`, `check_pupil_trials.py`;\noutputs `scan_all.csv`, `check_*.csv`).\n\n### Discrepancies Found\n| Topic | Code says | Data shows | Papers say | Resolution |\n|-------|-----------|------------|------------|------------|\n| Cache loading | `from_local_cache(use_static_cache=True)` needs `<dir>/visual-behavior-ophys/manifests` | cache has manifest json at top level + `visual-behavior-ophys-1.1.0/` data dir | tutorials use `from_s3_cache` (no network here) | Use `from_local_cache(cache_dir='/app/data', use_static_cache=False)`; verified all 284 NWBs are reachable through the SDK |\n| Dataset size | metadata tables cover 1936 experiments / 703 sessions / 133k cells | only 284 NWB files present (202 active) | paper analysed 376 imaging sessions / 82 mice | Local cache is a subset; absolute counts cannot match, **rates** must (and do). Restrict to experiments whose NWB exists |\n| Catch probability | trial types from `trial.py` | catch/(go+catch) = **0.1253** over 202 experiments | \"~12.5%\" (whitepaper) | Consistent |\n| Omission probability | `presentations.py` marks `omitted` | omitted fraction of change-detection flashes = **0.0352** (mean) | \"5% of image repeats were omitted\" | Consistent: 5% applies to *eligible* repeats only - changes, the flash before a change, and the first flashes after a change are never omitted, which lowers the overall rate to ~3.5% |\n| Change times | SWDB asserts change_times are a subset of flash start times | true in **202/202** experiments | change is a flash | Consistent |\n| Change time distribution | - | change-start: min 2.79 s, mean ~4.2 s | \"truncated exponential 2.25-8.25 s, mean 4.2 s\" | Consistent |\n| Images per session | - | exactly 8 non-omitted images in every experiment; 16 unique across the dataset (2 disjoint sets A/B) | \"Each session included 8 images\" | Consistent |\n| Ophys frame rate | - | 30.93-30.95 Hz (168 single-plane experiments), 10.73 Hz (34 mesoscope experiments) | \"31 Hz single plane, 11 Hz per plane multi-plane\" | Consistent |\n| Running speed | `running_processing.get_running_df` (zscore 10 outlier removal + Butterworth lowpass) | `running_speed` @59.95 Hz, no NaNs in any experiment | \"10 Hz lowpass Butterworth\" (code uses order 3, Wn=4 Hz, fs=60) | Use SDK `running_speed` as-is (already the processed, filtered stream) |\n| Eye tracking | `EyeTrackingTable.from_nwb` recomputes blinks and NaNs them | ~3.5% NaN frames/experiment; **3 sessions have no eye-tracking table at all** | \"eye tracking (30 Hz)\" | Blinks -> NaN by design. Handle explicitly (see Step 5 decisions) |\n| Neural signal | SDK provides `dff_traces`, `events`, `filtered_events` | both present for all experiments | \"For all analysis of neural data we used the detected calcium events\" (paper) | Use **`events`** (L0-detected calcium event magnitudes), matching the paper |\n| ROI curation | `exclude_invalid_rois=True` default | `cell_specimen_table.valid_roi` is True for 100% of returned ROIs | ROI FILTERING section | Nothing further to do - released NWBs contain only valid ROIs |\n| Multi-plane sessions | experiments = imaging planes | 6 sessions have 3-7 planes; planes in a session share *identical* trial tables (verified: 0/174 sessions disagree) | \"up to 8 imaging planes (8 experiments) per session\" | Merge planes of the same `ophys_session_id` into ONE decoder session (simultaneously recorded neurons) |\n| Trial outcome labels | `trial.py`: hit/miss/false_alarm/correct_reject defined only for go/catch | every go+catch trial has exactly one of the four | go/no-go task | Use the 4 outcomes as the static per-trial output |\n| Behaviour rates | - | hit rate 0.350, false-alarm rate 0.143 over all included trials | d-prime >= 1 QC criterion | Consistent with well-trained mice (d' ~ 0.5-1.5 range) |\n\nNo unresolved discrepancies.\n\n---\n\n## Step 5: Mapping Planning\n**Status**: COMPLETE\n\n### Session / trial definition\n- **Session** = one `ophys_session_id` (a single continuous recording). For Multiscope sessions the 3-7 simultaneously\n  recorded imaging planes (`ophys_experiment_id`s) are **concatenated along the neuron axis** into one session, because\n  they are simultaneously recorded neurons sharing one behavior session (verified: all planes of a session have identical\n  trial tables). Only **active behavior** sessions are used (passive OPHYS_2/OPHYS_5 have no choices/outcomes).\n- **Trial** = a `trials` row with `(go | catch) & ~aborted & ~auto_rewarded`, per the Decoder Task spec.\n- **Alignment event** = `trials.change_time` (the stimulus change on go trials, the sham change on catch trials).\n  This is the reference event used by the Allen SWDB analysis code (`save_trial_response_df.py`).\n- **Trial window** = **[-2.25 s, +3.75 s]** around the change time = 8 flash cycles (3 before, 5 after).\n  Data-driven justification: over all 202 active experiments min(change_time - start_time) = 2.79 s and\n  min(stop_time - change_time) = 4.20 s, so this window is **always inside the trial** (no bleed into neighbouring trials,\n  no truncation, identical length for every trial) and always inside `ophys_timestamps`.\n- **Time bin** = **250 ms** -> **24 bins/trial**. Chosen because (a) it equals the image presentation duration and divides\n  the 750 ms flash cycle exactly into 3, so image/change labels never straddle bins; (b) it is larger than the slowest\n  ophys frame interval (93 ms on the Mesoscope), so every bin of every session contains >= 2 imaging frames, allowing one\n  common bin size for all sessions as the format requires.\n\n### Variable Mapping\n| Source Variable | Target Field | Transform | Reference Code Function(s) | Notes |\n|-----------------|--------------|-----------|----------------------------|-------|\n| `experiment.events['events']` (L0 detected calcium events) + `ophys_timestamps` | `neural` | sum of event magnitudes of the ophys frames whose timestamp falls in each 250 ms bin -> (n_neurons, 24) float32 | `Events.from_nwb`; paper \"we used the detected calcium events\" | Neurons of all planes of a session stacked |\n| (none) | `input` | empty array (0, 24) float32, `input_names=[]` | - | Decoder Task specifies \"No inputs for this task\" |\n| `stimulus_presentations.image_name` (change_detection block, non-omitted) | `output[0]` image_identity | per bin: image of the most recent non-omitted flash onset <= bin centre; 16 global classes (im000...im106) | tutorials use `stimulus_block_name.str.contains('change_detection')` | Holding the identity across the 500 ms gray ISI implements the paper's \"image presentation interval\" (750 ms starting at each flash); during a 5% omission the identity of the ongoing (repeating) image is unchanged, so it is held |\n| `stimulus_presentations.is_change` | `output[1]` image_change | 1 in the bins of the presentation interval of a change flash (3 bins = 750 ms starting at the change), else 0 | `presentations.py` `is_change` | Catch trials have a *sham* change (`is_change` False) so they stay 0 - exactly the change-vs-repeat discrimination of the paper's change decoder |\n| `running_speed` (`speed`, 60 Hz) | `output[2]` running_speed_quintile | mean speed per 250 ms bin -> discretised into 5 **global** equal-percentile bins (20/40/60/80th pct of all bins in the dataset) | `running_processing.get_running_df` (already filtered by SDK) | Decoder Task: \"discretized into five equal percentile bins\" |\n| `eye_tracking.pupil_area` (30 Hz) | `output[3]` pupil_diameter_quintile | diameter = 2*sqrt(area/pi); blink NaN gaps <= 0.5 s linearly interpolated; mean per bin; 5 global equal-percentile bins | `EyeTrackingTable.from_nwb` (blinks already NaN, z=3, dilation 2) | Diameter (not area) as required |\n| `trials.hit/miss/false_alarm/correct_reject` | `output[4]` trial_outcome | static per trial, broadcast to all 24 bins; 4 classes | `trial.py::_get_trial_data` | Static per-trial output as specified |\n| `ophys_experiment_table.mouse_id` | `subjects`, `subject_idx` | str ids | - | |\n| `ophys_experiment_table.targeted_structure` (per plane) | `brain_regions`, `brain_region_idx` | VISp / VISl per neuron | - | |\n\n### Key Decisions\n1. **Neural signal = detected calcium events (`events`), summed per bin.** The paper states \"For all analysis of neural data\n   we used the detected calcium events\"; events remove the slow GCaMP6f decay, which otherwise smears stimulus information\n   across many 250 ms bins. Raw (unsmoothed) `events` is used rather than `filtered_events`, whose causal half-gaussian is\n   documented in the SDK as a *visualisation* smoother; binning already integrates over 250 ms.\n2. **dF/F is not recomputed** - the Allen pipeline already provides `dff_traces`, and the event detection was run on those\n   traces. ROI curation is likewise already applied (`valid_roi`, `exclude_invalid_rois=True` default): 100% of returned ROIs\n   are valid, so no further neuron filtering is warranted (and none is described in the papers).\n3. **Mesoscope planes merged per session** (simultaneously recorded populations) - gives larger neuron counts and matches the\n   whitepaper definition of a *session* vs an *experiment*.\n4. **Passive sessions excluded** (no behavioral report -> no trial outcome), matching the paper (\"passive viewing ... was not\n   analyzed here\").\n5. **Aborted and auto-rewarded trials excluded** per the Decoder Task spec; this also matches the SDK's own convention that\n   hit/miss/false-alarm/correct-reject are only defined for go/catch trials.\n6. **Global (dataset-wide) quintile edges** for running speed and pupil diameter, so the 5 classes are equally populated over\n   the whole dataset as \"five equal percentile bins\" requires, and so that one shared decoder head sees a consistent labelling.\n7. **Missing pupil data**: 3 of 174 active sessions have *no* eye-tracking table at all -> those sessions are dropped (a\n   required output cannot be fabricated). Within the remaining sessions, blink gaps <= 0.5 s are linearly interpolated (blinks\n   are short); trials that still contain a bin with no valid pupil sample (~6% of trials) are dropped. Fabricating pupil\n   values for a *decoded* variable would corrupt the evaluation.\n8. **All active sessions of all cre lines, both image sets, both areas and all experience levels are kept.** The paper's\n   restriction to familiar/Mesoscope sessions served its scientific question (strategy comparison); for building a decoding\n   dataset, more sessions is strictly better and the task says to convert data collected under the Visual Behavior task.\n9. **Image identity uses the 16 global image names**, not the per-session 0-7 `image_index`, because index i means different\n   images in image sets A and B.\n\n### Planned Sanity Checks\n- [ ] catch/(go+catch) ~ 0.125 (whitepaper ~12.5%)\n- [ ] 8 distinct images present per session; 16 across the dataset\n- [ ] change_time always coincides with a flash start time\n- [ ] image_change == 1 in exactly 3 bins (bins 9-11) of every go trial and 0 for every catch trial\n- [ ] image identity at bin 8 (just before change) != identity at bin 9 for go trials, and == for catch trials\n- [ ] running/pupil quintiles each ~20% of bins\n- [ ] trial-outcome distribution reproduces hit rate ~0.35, FA rate ~0.14\n- [ ] per-neuron binned event sums match an independent re-computation directly from the SDK (spot check)\n- [ ] no NaN/Inf anywhere; all trials have identical shape (n_neurons, 24)\n- [ ] number of trials per session equals the number of go+catch trials in the SDK trials table (minus pupil-dropped trials)\n\n---\n\n## Step 6: Script Development\n**Status**: COMPLETE\n\n`/app/convert_data.py` (runs as `python -u /app/convert_data.py <outfile> [--full|--sample] [--show-processing]`).\n\nStructure:\n- `get_cache()` / `available_experiment_table()` - open the AllenSDK `VisualBehaviorOphysProjectCache`\n  (`from_local_cache(cache_dir='/app/data', use_static_cache=False)`) and restrict the experiment table to experiments\n  whose NWB file is present locally. **All data access goes through the SDK**; no NWB file is opened directly.\n- `process_session((session_id, experiment_ids, region_map, want_debug, signal))` - worker that loads every imaging plane of\n  one ophys session, selects go+catch trials, and bins neural + behaviour into the 24 x 250 ms trial windows.\n- `bin_sum` / `bin_mean_nan` - fully vectorised binning via cumulative sums + `np.searchsorted` (no python loop over trials);\n  `bin_mean_nan` propagates NaN only when a bin contains *no* valid sample.\n- `interpolate_short_gaps` - linear interpolation of pupil blink gaps <= 0.5 s.\n- `quantile_bins` / `digitize_with` - global equal-percentile discretisation of running speed and pupil diameter.\n- `plot_processing` - `--show-processing` figure with 6 rows x 3 trials: raw events/dF/F traces, binned neural matrix,\n  stimulus flashes vs decoded image identity, image-change binary vs the true change flash, raw running speed vs its\n  quintile, raw pupil diameter vs its quintile with licks/rewards and the trial outcome.\n- `main()` - grouping by `ophys_session_id`, multiprocessing pool, global quantile edges, assembly of the output dict,\n  statistics printout, pickling.\n\nCode inefficiencies identified:\n- A naive implementation would slice traces per trial in a Python loop (n_trials x n_cells slices).\n- Re-opening the cache per experiment is expensive.\n\nCode speedups added:\n- Cumulative-sum + `searchsorted` binning: all trials of a session are binned in one vectorised call.\n- One cache handle per worker process, sessions processed in parallel (`--nproc`, default 16).\n- `float32` storage for neural data.\n- Timing is printed per run (`s/session`).\n\nExtra option `--neural-signal {dff,events,filtered_events}` was added so the choice of neural signal could be tested\nempirically (see Step 7/8).\n\n---\n\n## Step 7: Sample Conversion and Validation\n**Status**: COMPLETE\n\n`python -u /app/convert_data.py /app/sample_data.pkl --sample --show-processing` -> `/app/conversion_sample_out.txt`\n`python -u /app/train_decoder.py /app/sample_data.pkl --verify-only` -> `/app/verification_sample_out.txt`\n(sample = one single-plane session 775289198 and one 7-plane Mesoscope session 951410079).\n\n### Sample Statistics","numLines":320,"startLine":1,"totalLines":622}}}
